The latter allows for more flexible modeling of the inter-task correlations by composing multiple features. We derive uniform error bounds for vector-valued functions sampled from a Gaussian process with a linear model of co-reginalization kernel. Furthermore, we show the potential improvement of performance using linear models of co-reginalization in a numerical comparison on a safe multi-task Bayesian optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [150] [MPINeuralODE: Multiple-Initial-Condition Physics-Informed Neural ODEs for Globally Consistent Dynamical System Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13305)
*Lake Yang,Antonio Malpica-Morales,Frank Ioannis Papadakis Wood,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPINeuralODE方法，结合物理信息残差和多重初始条件多打靶课程，在Lotka-Volterra上实现优于基线Neural ODE的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Neural ODE拟合训练轨迹时，对未见初始条件和长期预测的泛化能力差。

Method: 结合软物理信息残差（PI）与多重初始条件（MIC）多打靶课程，两者结构互补：物理项约束向量场幅度，MIC扩大支持域。

Result: 在Lotka-Volterra上，MPINeuralODE的样本外和长期MSE最低，比基线Neural ODE降低26%，哈密顿漂移与PINN消融相当。

Conclusion: MPINeuralODE能更有效地恢复底层向量场，提升泛化、长期稳定性和物理一致性。

Abstract: Neural ordinary differential equations (Neural ODEs) often fit training trajectories while generalizing poorly to unseen initial conditions and long horizons. We propose MPINeuralODE, which combines a soft physics-informed residual with a Multiple-Initial-Condition (MIC) multiple-shooting curriculum whose ingredients are structurally complementary: the physics term anchors the vector-field magnitude on the support that MIC enlarges. We evaluate along three axes: out-of-sample error, long-horizon stability, and Hamiltonian drift, which together expose whether the learned dynamics recover the underlying vector field. On Lotka-Volterra, MPINeuralODE achieves the lowest out-of-sample and long-horizon MSE among data-driven methods, with a 26% reduction over the baseline Neural ODE, while essentially matching the PINN ablation on Hamiltonian drift.

</details>


### [151] [Shortcut Mitigation via Spurious-Positive Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13340)
*Phuong Quynh Le,Jörg Schlötterer,Sari Sadiya,Gemma Roig,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外标注或平衡数据的快捷特征缓解方法，通过识别模型依赖虚假属性的关键神经元并正则化其影响，迫使模型学习更具信息量的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快捷特征缓解方法依赖训练数据标注、组平衡的保留数据或训练数据中存在所有属性-类别组合，这些条件在实际中很少满足，因此需要一种更实用的方法。

Method: 首先通过目标模型分析识别模型依赖虚假属性的少量实例，然后基于"该特征不应被用于预测"的推理，定位中间层中高度相关的神经元，并对这些神经元进行正则化以削弱其影响。

Result: 模型学会依赖信息性特征而非因错误原因预测正确，鲁棒性得到提升，且无需额外的平衡保留数据或标注。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种实用且高效的快捷特征缓解方案，降低了对外部资源的依赖。

Abstract: Shortcut mitigation strategies commonly rely on training data annotations, group-balanced held-out data or the presence of all groups, i.e., all combinations of (spurious) attributes and classes, in the training data. However, these requirements are rarely met in practice. We instead propose a method for targeted model analysis to identify a small set of instances in which the model relies on spurious attributes. Using that set and following ``this feature should not be used for prediction'' reasoning, we identify highly relevant neurons in an intermediate layer and regularize their impact. This ensures that models learn to depend on informative features rather than being right for the wrong reasons, thereby improving robustness without requiring additional balanced held-out data or annotations.

</details>


### [152] [Contextual Bandits for Resource-Constrained Devices using Probabilistic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13346)
*Marco Angioli,Kevin Johansson,Antonello Rosato,Amy Loutfi,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概率超维CB算法，通过概率更新规则替代确定性累积，在低精度条件下防止溢出并降低更新成本，在3位精度下接近全精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性CB算法在资源受限设备上计算和内存成本高；HD-CB虽更优但累积式学习需要高精度，周期性二值化又会损失幅度信息，降低决策质量。

Method: 提出概率HD-CB，采用概率更新规则：每步仅随机更新向量子集（时间衰减概率），组件值限制在[-k,+k]范围内，实现低精度、防溢出、降低预期更新成本。

Result: 在Open Bandit Pipeline标准化合成CB基准上的离策略评估显示，概率HD-CB在同等精度下持续优于二值化HD-CB，且使用3位组件时接近全精度HD-CB的性能。

Conclusion: 概率HD-CB是一种适合资源受限设备的低精度CB方案，在内存效率和计算成本之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Contextual bandits (CB) are online sequential decision-making problems under partial feedback that underpin many adaptive services. There is a growing demand to deploy CB agents directly on-device, under strict constraints on memory, compute, and energy. However, standard linear CB algorithms are often impractical for resource-constrained devices with their unfavorable scaling in computational and memory costs. Recently, HD-CB, a CB approach based on hyperdimensional computing principles, has been proposed to model and solve CB problems by moving into high-dimensional spaces. HD-CB offers faster convergence, favorable scalability, and improves memory efficiency compared to linear CB algorithms. However, its learning rule is accumulation-based: the values of action vectors grow over time, requiring high precision. While periodic binarization can prevent overflow in low-precision components, it may discard important information about magnitudes and degrade decision quality. This paper introduces probabilistic HD-CB, a low-precision variant that replaces deterministic accumulation with a probabilistic update rule. At each step, only a random subset of vector components is updated, with a time-decaying update probability, and component values are constrained to a predefined range [-k,+k]. This approach enables low-precision components, prevents overflow without periodic binarization, and reduces the expected update cost in proportion to the fraction of updated components. Off-policy evaluation on standardized synthetic CB benchmarks using the Open Bandit Pipeline shows that probabilistic HD-CB consistently outperforms binarized HD-CB at equal precision, while approaching the performance of HD-CB with as few as 3 bits per component.

</details>


### [153] [GeoFlowVLM: Geometry-Aware Joint Uncertainty for Frozen Vision-Language Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13352)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Li Ju,Andreas Hellander,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoFlowVLM，一种后验适配器，通过在乘积超球面上进行黎曼流匹配，从单一模型中同时提取偶发不确定性和认知不确定性，分别用于跨模态检索校准和零样本分类校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双编码器视觉-语言模型通过ℓ2归一化将图像和文本映射到确定性点，既不暴露偶发不确定性（跨模态歧义）也不暴露认知不确定性（训练分布外支持不足）。现有的后验方法要么只恢复两种不确定性中的一种，要么忽略这些模型嵌入的超球面几何结构。

Method: 提出GeoFlowVLM，在乘积超球面S^(d-1)×S^(d-1)上通过单掩码速度场的黎曼流匹配学习配对ℓ2归一化双编码器VLM嵌入的联合分布。从该单一模型中推导出两个量：条件检索熵（量化偶发歧义性）和边际典型性认知评分（通过联合NLL的链式分解得到）。

Result: 在三个检索基准上，熵指标在双向Recall@1上具有接近理想的单调校准；在四个零样本分类基准上，边际典型性求和产生一致校准的选择性准确率。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够统一地表征双编码器VLM中的两种不确定性来源，并在跨模态检索和零样本分类任务上均取得良好的校准效果。

Abstract: Standard dual-encoder vision-language models that map images and text to deterministic points on a shared unit hypersphere through $\ell_2$ normalization typically expose neither \emph{aleatoric} uncertainty (cross-modal ambiguity) nor \emph{epistemic} uncertainty (lack of training-distribution support). Existing post-hoc methods either recover at most one of the two uncertainty components, or ignore the hyperspherical geometry of these models' embeddings. We propose \textbf{GeoFlowVLM} as a post-hoc adapter that learns the joint distribution of paired $\ell_2$-normalised dual-encoder VLM embeddings on the product hypersphere $\mathbb{S}^{d-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ via Riemannian flow matching with a single masked velocity field. A consistency result shows that, in the population limit, the trained network exposes the joint flow and both cross-modal conditional flows as valid Riemannian flow-matching velocity fields on their respective domains. We derive two quantities from this single model: a conditional retrieval entropy that quantifies aleatoric ambiguity with a decision-theoretic interpretation via a Fano-type bound, and a marginal-typicality epistemic score justified by an exact chain-rule decomposition of the joint NLL. This decomposition isolates a cross-modal pointwise-mutual-information term that is structurally discriminative rather than epistemic, and is empirically the only consistently uninformative standalone component. Empirically, the entropy tracks Recall@1 with near-ideal monotonic calibration across three retrieval benchmarks in both directions, and the marginal-typicality sum yields consistently calibrated selective accuracy across four zero-shot classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [154] [Building Interactive Real-Time Agents with Asynchronous I/O and Speculative Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13360)
*Coleman Hooper,Minwoo Kang,Suhong Moon,Nicholas Lee,Eric Wen,John Wawrzynek,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Amir Gholami,Kurt Keutzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对需要实时交互的AI Agent场景，提出异步I/O和推测性工具调用两种方法，以及针对边缘模型基于时钟的训练方法，显著降低工具调用延迟（1.3-2.2倍加速），同时保持较高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent在复杂多轮工具调用场景下会产生数秒甚至更长的延迟，无法满足语音交互等实时应用对亚秒级延迟的要求，因此需要降低推理和工具执行带来的延迟。

Method: 1) 异步I/O：将Agent"推理与行动"线程与等待外部信息（用户或环境）解耦，在等待时重叠执行Agent处理；2) 推测性工具调用：在Agent不确定是否收集到完整信息时，提前推测性执行任务；3) 基于时钟的训练方法：针对边缘小模型，使其适应流式输入和异步响应，配合SFT合成数据策略。

Result: 在云端模型上实现1.3-1.7倍加速且精度损失小；在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct边缘模型上实现1.6-2.2倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法无需修改现有实时云API即可直接应用，同时为边缘小模型提供了实时交互能力，有效解决了Agent工具调用场景下的延迟瓶颈问题。

Abstract: There is a growing demand for agentic AI technologies for a range of downstream applications like customer service and personal assistants. For applications where the agent needs to interact with a person, real-time low-latency responsiveness is required; for example, with voice-controlled applications, under 1 second of latency is typically required for the interaction to feel seamless. However, if we want the LLM to reason and execute an agentic workflow with tool calling, this can add can add several seconds or more of latency, which is prohibitive for real-time latency-sensitive applications. In our work, we aim to enable real-time interaction even for agents with complex multi-turn tool calling. We propose Asynchronous I/O, which decouples the core agent reason-and-act thread from waiting for additional information from either the user or environment, thereby allowing for overlapping agentic processing while waiting on external delays. We also propose Speculative Tool Calling as a method to manage task execution when the agent is still unsure if it has received the full information or if additional user information may later be provided. For strong cloud models, our method can be applied out-of-the-box to existing real-time cloud APIs, providing 1.3-1.7$\times$ speedups with minor accuracy loss. To enable real-time interaction with small edge-scale models, we also present a clock-based training methodology that adapts the model to handle streaming inputs and asynchronous responses, and demonstrate a synthetic data generation strategy for SFT. Altogether, this approach provides 1.6-2.2$\times$ speedups with the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct models across multiple tool calling benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Q-Flow: Stable and Expressive Reinforcement Learning with Flow-Based Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13435)
*JaeHyeok Doo,Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,Kimin Lee,Minjoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Flow通过利用流模型的确定性动力学将终端轨迹价值显式传播到中间潜在状态，在保持高表达能力的同时实现稳定的策略优化，在OGBench基准上平均超越SOTA 10.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的模型在强化学习中具有高表达能力，但梯度优化需要反向传播通过数值求解器，导致不稳定；现有方法通过限制表达能力来优化稳定性，造成了表达灵活性与优化稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: 提出Q-Flow框架，利用流动力学的确定性特性，将终端轨迹价值显式传播到策略诱导流中的中间潜在状态，从而在不展开数值求解器的情况下，使用中间价值梯度进行稳定的策略优化。

Result: 在OGBench基准上，Q-Flow持续超越SOTA基线平均10.6个百分点，并在同一框架下实现了稳定的在线适应。

Conclusion: Q-Flow成功弥合了基于流的策略在优化稳定性与表达能力之间的差距，为高容量流模型在强化学习中的高效利用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: There is growing interest in utilizing flow-based models as decision-making policies in reinforcement learning due to their high expressive capacity. However, effectively leveraging this expressivity for value maximization remains challenging, as naive gradient-based optimization requires backpropagating through numerical solvers and often leads to instability. Existing approaches typically address this issue by restricting the expressive capacity of flow-based policies, resulting in a trade-off between optimization stability and representational flexibility. To resolve this, we introduce Q-Flow, a framework that leverages the deterministic nature of flow dynamics to explicitly propagate terminal trajectory value to intermediate latent states along the policy-induced flow. This formulation enables stable policy optimization using intermediate value gradients without unrolling the numerical solver, effectively bridging the gap between stability and expressivity. We evaluate Q-Flow in the offline learning setting on the challenging OGBench suite, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 10.6 percentage points, while also enabling stable online adaptation within the same framework.

</details>


### [156] [Discovery of Hidden Miscalibration Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13484)
*Katarzyna Kobalczyk,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种发现模型隐藏的误校准区域的方法，通过学习校准感知的输入表示和核平滑估计局部误校准，揭示了LLM中普遍存在的输入依赖校准异质性，并可用于局部置信度校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估仅基于置信度分数，忽略了模型在不同输入上的系统过/欠置信问题，导致全局可靠性指标掩盖局部校准失败。

Method: 定义误校准场，学习校准感知的输入空间表示，通过核平滑在学习的几何空间中估计带符号的局部误校准。

Result: 在4个真实世界LLM基准和12个LLM上发现输入依赖的校准异质性普遍存在；发现的误校准场可用于局部置信度校正，在系统误校准区域优于等渗回归和温度缩放。

Conclusion: 全局校准评估存在局限性，所提框架能有效发现并利用局部误校准结构进行可操作的置信度校正。

Abstract: Calibration is commonly evaluated by comparing model confidence with its empirical correctness, implicitly treating reliability as a function of the confidence score alone. However, this view can hide substantial structure: models may be systematically overconfident on some kinds of inputs and underconfident on others, causing global reliability diagnostics to obscure localised calibration failures. To address this, we formulate the problem of discovering hidden miscalibration regimes without assuming access to predefined data slices. We define the corresponding miscalibration field and propose a diagnostic framework for estimating it. Our approach learns a calibration-aware representation of the input space and estimates signed local miscalibration by kernel smoothing in the learned geometry. Across four real-world LLM benchmarks and twelve LLMs, we find that input-dependent calibration heterogeneity is prevalent. We further show that the discovered fields are actionable: they support local confidence correction and reduce calibration error in systematically miscalibrated regions where confidence-based methods such as isotonic regression and temperature scaling are less effective.

</details>


### [157] [Teaching and Learning under Deductive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13384)
*Jan Arne Telle,Brigt Håvardstun,Jose Hernandez-Orallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个考虑演绎错误的教学与学习框架，在PAC设置下研究了教师如何找到高概率导致学习者猜测近似正确假设的教学集，并分析了六种相关问题的计算复杂度，最后通过实验研究了最能代表LLM教学行为的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器教学与学习模型假设学习者在内部演绎推理中不会出错，但人类和少样本学习中的大语言模型并不满足这一假设。它们会随机性地在某些一致性检查上失败，因此需要一个新的框架来考虑这些演绎错误。

Method: 在PAC（可能近似正确）设置下，研究者将学习者的演绎错误纳入考量，定义了教师需要找到的PAC教学集。通过理论分析，研究了六种不同问题的计算复杂度，给出了以教学集大小为参数的XP算法，并在标准复杂度假设下证明了紧确的运行时间界限。同时进行了小规模实验研究。

Result: 给出了以教学集大小为参数的XP算法，并在标准复杂度假设（如ETH）下证明了紧确的运行时间界限。实验部分揭示了哪些教学与学习协议最能代表LLM教学会话中观察到的行为。

Conclusion: 该框架为存在演绎错误的学习者（如人类和LLM）提供了理论支撑，对理解如何在错误存在的情况下设计有效的教学策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: Most models of machine teaching and learning assume the learner makes no errors in its internal deductive inference. However, humans and large language models in few-shot learning regimes are two important examples of learners where this does not hold. They fail on some consistency checks, and they can fail stochastically. In this paper we introduce a teaching and learning framework that takes these deductive errors into account. We specifically study the case of machine teaching, as different characterizations of the teacher can account for both machine teaching and learning. In an overhauled Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) setting, we study theoretically that, for some estimated error level, the teacher must find a PAC teaching set that with high probability will lead the learner to guess a hypothesis that is approximately correct. We study the computational complexity of six different problems related to computing optimal PAC teaching sets. We give XP algorithms parametrized by size of teaching set, with tight runtime bounds under standard complexity assumptions like ETH. These results are complemented with a small experimental study of which teaching and learning protocols can best represent the observed behavior in some LLM teaching sessions.

</details>


### [158] [Support-Conditioned Flow Matching Is Kernel Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13386)
*Daniel Matsui Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在最优传输路径下，条件生成模型中的交叉注意力机制等价于Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器，核宽度随流时间递减；理论预测了三种失败模式，实验验证了学习式条件化能改善这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示交叉注意力条件化与经典核方法之间的理论联系，解释其工作原理及失败原因。

Method: 在高斯最优传输路径下，推导有限支撑集诱导的精确速度场等价于Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器；通过理论分析预测三种失败模式，并在高斯混合、球壳和DINOv2 ImageNet特征上验证。

Result: 单高斯核注意力头精确计算该速度场；三种失败模式（高维最近邻崩塌、各向同性核与数据几何不匹配、非参数估计支撑不足）均被实验验证；IP-Adapter的交叉注意力在实践中近似实现了NW平滑。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力条件化与核平滑理论深度关联，学习式条件化能有效弥补非参数方法的固有缺陷。

Abstract: Generative models are often conditioned on a small set of examples via cross-attention. Under the Gaussian optimal-transport path, we show that the exact velocity field induced by a finite support set is a Nadaraya--Watson kernel smoother whose bandwidth decreases with flow time, from broad averaging at early steps to nearest-neighbor at late steps. A single Gaussian-kernel attention head exactly computes this field, connecting cross-attention conditioning to classical kernel theory. The theory predicts three failure regimes: nearest-neighbor collapse of the kernel at high dimension, mismatch between the isotropic kernel and the data geometry, and insufficient support for nonparametric estimation. Experiments on Gaussian mixtures, spherical shells, and DINOv2 ImageNet features confirm that learned conditioning improves in precisely these regimes, and that IP-Adapter's cross-attention implements approximate NW smoothing in practice.

</details>


### [159] [Taming the Long Tail: Rebalancing Adversarial Training via Adaptive Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13395)
*Lilin Zhang,Yimo Guo,Yue Li,Jiancheng Shi,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustLT框架，通过自适应调整对抗训练中的扰动，同时解决长尾数据下的对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练主要针对平衡数据集，忽视了现实世界中长尾数据带来的挑战。对抗样本中的扰动会改变训练分布，但这一特性尚未被充分利用来解决长尾学习中的类别不平衡问题。

Method: RobustLT是一种即插即用的框架，通过理论分析揭示对抗训练在长尾数据上的两个关键局限：类别不平衡导致的偏斜训练目标，以及对抗分布的不稳定演化。该方法自适应调整对抗训练中的扰动，同时兼顾对抗鲁棒性和类别平衡。

Result: 大量实验表明，RobustLT在长尾数据集上一致性地提升了对抗鲁棒性和类别平衡性能。

Conclusion: 对抗扰动不仅能提升模型鲁棒性，还能缓解类别不平衡问题，RobustLT有效结合了这两点优势。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are highly vulnerable to adversarial examples, i.e.,small perturbations that can significantly degrade model performance. While adversarial training has become the primary defense strategy, most studies focus on balanced datasets, overlooking the challenges posed by real-world long-tail data. Motivated by the fact that perturbations in adversarial examples inherently alter the training distribution, we theoretically investigate their impact. We first revisit adversarial training for long-tail data and identify two key limitations: (i) a skewed training objective caused by class imbalance, and (ii) unstable evolution of adversarial distributions. Furthermore, we show that perturbations can simultaneously address both adversarial vulnerability and class imbalance. Based on these insights, we propose RobustLT, a plug-and-play framework that adaptively adjusts perturbations during adversarial training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RobustLT consistently enhances adversarial robustness and class-balance on long-tailed datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}.

</details>


### [160] [The Diffusion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13399)
*Akhil Premkumar,Sarah Lucioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用扩散模型表达能力的新型编码器，通过交替训练方案解决编码器与解码器之间的同步问题，同时保留标准扩散模型的简单高效训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分自编码器（VAE）中，重参数化技巧限制了编码器只能使用简单的分布族，无法充分利用扩散模型的强大表达能力。作者希望用扩散模型替换编码器，同时解决编码器与解码器之间潜在表示更新方向相反的问题。

Method: 提出一种交替训练方案，受期望最大化（EM）算法启发。编码器和解码器交替更新对潜在表示的内部估计，实现可靠同步，同时保持标准扩散模型的训练目标。

Result: 该方法实现了编码器与解码器之间更可靠的同步，解决了二者更新方向冲突的问题。

Conclusion: 提出的交替训练方案有效弥合了扩散模型与传统VAE编码器之间的差距，为构建更强大的生成模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: We construct a new kind of encoder, leveraging the expressive power of diffusion models. In a traditional variational autoencoder, the encoder and decoder jointly negotiate a latent representation of the input. This is made possible by the reparameterization trick, which simplifies training at the cost of restricting the encoder to a simple family of distributions. Replacing this encoder with a diffusion model requires rethinking how the decoder pressure can be transmitted back to the encoder, given that they tend to update their internal estimates of the latent in opposing directions. We solve this problem with an alternating training scheme, inspired by the expectation-maximization algorithm. Our method enables more reliable synchronization between encoder and decoder, while preserving the simple and efficient training objective of standard diffusion models.

</details>


### [161] [Trajectory-Level Data Augmentation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13401)
*Tobias Schmähling,Matthias Burkhardt,Tobias Windisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向离线强化学习的数据增强方法，利用轨迹结构与几何关系，从少量次优轨迹中提升数据质量与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线强化学习中从少量次优轨迹训练模型的问题，提升数据利用效率与模型性能。

Method: 提出基于轨迹的数据增强技术，利用任务结构、奖励与价值函数的几何关系以及日志策略的数学性质，支持次优日志策略下的数据收集与增强。

Result: 在多种维度的定位任务及部分可观测条件下，该方法提升了离线强化学习的性能。

Conclusion: 所提数据增强方法在理论和实证上均有效，能够从少量次优轨迹中提升离线强化学习效果。

Abstract: We propose a data augmentation method for offline reinforcement learning, motivated by active positioning problems. Particularly, our approach enables the training of off-policy models from a limited number of suboptimal trajectories. We introduce a trajectory-based augmentation technique that exploits task structure and the geometric relationship between rewards, value functions, and mathematical properties of logging policies. During data collection, our augmentation supports suboptimal logging policies, leading to higher data quality and improved offline reinforcement learning performance. We provide theoretical justification for these strategies and validate them empirically across positioning tasks of varying dimensionality and under partial observability.

</details>


### [162] [Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540)
*Haonan Yuan,Qingyun Sun,Junhua Shi,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyGFM是首个多领域动态图基础模型，通过解耦语义-时间双分支预训练、散度感知的跨域路由机制和散度条件化提示生成，有效解决了跨域负迁移问题，在节点分类和链接预测任务上超越12个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图模型在跨域预训练时面临严重的负知识迁移问题，因为不同领域的动态图语义和时间模式天然不一致，导致"预训练-微调"范式效果不佳，且缺乏统一的多领域动态图基础模型。

Method: 提出双分支预训练策略实现语义-时间解耦；设计散度感知的跨域路由机制缓解负迁移；开发散度条件化提示生成器实现高效微调。

Result: 在连续动态图基准测试中，DyGFM在节点分类和链接预测任务上一致超越12个SOTA基线，兼具优越的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: DyGFM通过解耦和散度条件化提示成功实现了多领域动态图的统一建模，为动态图基础模型构建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are ubiquitous in real-world systems, and building generalizable dynamic Graph Foundation Models has become a frontier in graph learning. However, dynamic graphs from different domains pose fundamental challenges to unified modeling, as their semantic and temporal patterns are inherently inconsistent, making the multi-domain pre-training difficult. Consequently, the widely used "pretrain-then-finetune" paradigm often suffers from severe negative knowledge transfer. To the best of our knowledge, there exists no multi-domain dynamic GFM. In this work, we propose DyGFM, a Dynamic Graph Foundation Model over multiple domains based on decoupled and divergence-conditioned prompting. To disentangle transferable semantics from the domain-specific dynamics, we introduce a dual-branch pre-training strategy with semantic-temporal decoupling. To alleviate negative transfer during domain adaptation, we further develop a cross-domain routing mechanism with divergence-aware expert selection. To enable efficient downstream fine-tuning, we design a divergence-conditioned prompt generator that injects lightweight, learnable graph prompts tailored to semantic and temporal traits. Extensive experiments on continuous dynamic graph benchmarks demonstrate that DyGFM consistently outperforms 12 state-of-the-art baselines on both node classification and link prediction tasks, achieving superior effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [163] [When is Warmstarting Effective for Scaling Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13405)
*Neeratyoy Mallik,Maciej Janowski,Johannes Hog,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Josif Grabocka,Frank Hutter,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从检查点进行模型增长（warmstarting）的实用边界，发现保留小模型初始性能并非必要，简单的架构无关增长策略优于复杂算子，并首次识别出增长因子g的上限——当超过2×时，从头训练更高效。实验在密集MLP和语言模型上验证，2×增长因子在20 tokens/参数预算下加速最显著，随预算增大收益递减，并提供了缩放定律作为实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型增长（warmstarting）在大规模训练中实践应用有限，作者认为这源于两个被低估的因素：(1) 过度强调保持小模型初始性能，限制了新架构的算子设计；(2) 对增长与超参数、缩放行为的相互作用分析不足，且文献中增长因子不一致。

Method: 通过广泛的消融实验，在密集MLP和密集语言模型上比较不同增长策略和增长因子g的效果，拟合缩放定律来量化增长收益与预算的关系，并重新审视已有文献中被忽略的增长上限现象。

Result: (1) 保留基模型初始性能并非最终性能的必要条件，简单架构无关策略优于复杂warmstarting算子；(2) 识别出增长因子g存在上限，超过此上限从头训练更高效；(3) 2×增长因子在收敛加速上最可靠，优势在20 tokens/参数预算下最显著，随预算增大收益递减；(4) 该上限在已有文献中也存在但未被报告。

Conclusion: 为模型增长实践提供了明确的经验边界和实用指南：不应过度追求初始性能保留，2×是最可靠的增长因子，并给出了何时增长（预算有限时）以及增长多少的缩放定律预测指导。

Abstract: Model growth from a given checkpoint aims to accelerate training of a larger model, offering potential resource savings. Despite recent interest, warmstarting has seen limited practical adoption in large-scale training. We attribute this to two underexplored factors: (1) an overemphasis on preserving the smaller model's performance at initialization, which constrains operator design for new architectures, and (2) insufficient analysis of how growth interacts with hyperparameters and scaling behavior, compounded by inconsistent growth factors across the literature.
  We show that preserving the base model's initial post-growth performance is not necessary for strong final performance, and that simple, architecture-agnostic growth strategies can outperform more complex warmstarting operators. Crucially, we empirically identify an upper bound on the growth factor $g$ beyond which training from scratch is more efficient. We observe this across multiple ablation setups. Notably, this limit is also present, but unreported, in prior published results. Across our experiments on dense MLPs and dense language models, we find that a $2\times$ growth factor is the most reliable in yielding convergence speedups, with gains most pronounced under 20 tokens/parameter budgets and diminishing as budget increases. We fit scaling laws over these observations to provide predictive guidance for practitioners deciding when and how much to grow. Together, our analysis provides practical guidelines and empirical limits for model growth.

</details>


### [164] [Self-Supervised On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13554)
*Asim Osman,Sasha Abramowitz,Mark Bergh,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Ruan John de Kock,Omayma Mahjoub,Oussama Hidaoui,Noah De Nicola,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Felix Chalumeau,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Siddarth Singh,Refiloe Shabe,Juan Claude Formanek,Simon Verster Du Toit,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPPO，一种基于on-policy的对比强化学习算法，无需奖励函数或经验回放，在连续/离散、单/多智能体任务中显著优于现有CRL方法，并在多数任务上匹敌甚至超越使用手工奖励的PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比强化学习（CRL）算法均依赖off-policy优化，且大多局限于连续动作空间，缺乏在离散环境中的研究，导致CRL与现代on-policy训练管线脱节。

Method: 提出Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation (CPPO)，一种on-policy对比RL算法，从对比Q值中推导策略优势，通过标准PPO目标进行优化，无需奖励函数或经验回放缓冲区。

Result: 在18个任务中，CPPO在14个任务上显著优于之前的CRL基线，并在12个任务上匹敌或超越使用手工密集奖励的PPO。

Conclusion: CPPO成功建立了CRL与on-policy训练管线的首个连接，证明了无需奖励函数的对比学习在on-policy框架下的有效性。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) learns goal-conditioned Q-values through a contrastive objective over state-action and goal representations, removing the need for hand-crafted reward functions. Despite impressive success in achieving viable self-supervised learning in RL, all existing CRL algorithms rely on off-policy optimisation and are mostly constrained to continuous action spaces, with little research invested in discrete environments. This leaves CRL disconnected from widely used and effective, modern on-policy training pipelines adopted across both single-agent and multi-agent RL in continuous and discrete environments. To establish a first connection, we introduce Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation (CPPO). CPPO is an on-policy contrastive RL algorithm that derives policy advantages directly from contrastive Q-values and optimises them via the standard PPO objective, without requiring a reward function or a replay buffer. We evaluate CPPO across continuous and discrete, single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks. Whilst the existence of an on-policy approach is inherently useful, we observe that \textbf{CPPO not only significantly outperforms the previous CRL baselines in 14 out of 18 tasks, but also matches or exceeds PPO's performance, which uses hand-crafted dense rewards, in 12 out of the 18 tasks tested.}

</details>


### [165] [Dynamical Predictive Modelling of Cardiovascular Disease Progression Post-Myocardial Infarction via ECG-Trained Artificial Intelligence Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13568)
*Riccardo Cavarra,Lupo Lovatelli,Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt,Shahid Aziz,Adelaide De Vecchi,Andrew King,Oleg Aslanidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合对比学习与监督多任务头的预训练AI模型，通过患者特定时间信息的心电图建模，在有限数据下显著提升了心梗后不良结局预测性能（AUC从0.608提升至0.794）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心梗致死率高，现有心电图预后模型因缺乏大规模标注数据而表现不佳，基础模型虽可从无标注数据中学习，但医学相关的训练策略尚未充分探索。

Method: 采用对比学习与监督多任务头结合的方式，利用患者特定的时间信息进行预训练，然后针对心梗后结局预测进行微调。

Result: 提出模型AUC达0.794，显著优于从头训练的模型（0.608 AUC）。

Conclusion: 临床结构化的心电图建模能在有限数据条件下有效提升分类性能。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) is a leading cause of death, and its adverse outcomes are urgent to predict. Yet ECG-based prognostic models underperform because deep learning requires large, labelled datasets, which are scarce in medicine. Foundation models can learn from unlabelled ECGs via selfsupervision, but medically relevant training strategies remain underexplored. We propose a pretrained artificial intelligence model that combines patient-specific temporal information using contrastive learning with supervised multitask heads, then fine-tunes on post-MI outcome prediction. The proposed model outperformed a model trained from scratch (0.794 vs 0.608 AUC) showing that clinically structured ECG modelling improves classification in limited data regimes.

</details>


### [166] [A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687)
*Jason Gaitonde,Frederic Koehler,Elchanan Mossel,Joonhyung Shin,Allan Sly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一类具有层次结构的合成语言，通过精确k-gram假设分析自回归生成中上下文长度与推理的作用，证明忠实采样长度n的序列需要Ω(n)的上下文长度，而仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆的推理模型可实现指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自回归生成中，上下文长度和推理能力的作用尚未被精确分析，本文旨在通过合成语言构建可解析的理论框架来量化二者对生成质量的影响。

Method: 引入基于树上广播过程的层次结构合成语言族；提出精确k-gram假设替代上下文长度为k的Transformer并通过实验验证；在两个设置（伊辛广播过程与染色广播过程）中推导生成序列的渐近统计量；对比有限上下文自回归模型与具备推理能力的模型的采样复杂度。

Result: 伊辛广播过程：生成和的方差随上下文深度对数线性增长，峰度收敛到高斯分布，均偏离真实语言；染色广播过程（冻结区）：有界上下文自回归高概率生成与任何有效着色不一致的序列；严格证明忠实采样需Ω(n)上下文；推理模型仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆即可精确采样；实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 有限上下文自回归生成在层次结构化语言上存在根本性局限，而引入推理能力（工作记忆）可实现指数级的采样效率提升，为理解Transformer架构中上下文长度与推理的权衡提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We introduce a family of synthetic languages with hierarchical structure -- generated by a broadcast process on trees -- for which the role of context length and reasoning in autoregressive generation can be analyzed precisely. At the heart of our analytic approach is an \emph{exact $k$-gram ansatz} in place of transformers with context length $k$, a substitution we then validate empirically. Using this ansatz we derive explicit asymptotic predictions for distributional statistics of the sequences produced by a trained model, instantiated in two settings. For the \emph{Ising broadcast process} (a soft-constrained language), we prove that the variance of the generated sum scales log-linearly in the context depth and its kurtosis converges to that of a Gaussian -- both deviating from the true language for any sublinear context. For the \emph{coloring broadcast process} (a hard-constrained language) in the freezing regime, bounded-context autoregression produces sequences that, with high probability, are inconsistent with \emph{any} valid coloring of the underlying tree. Together these results imply an $Ω(n)$ lower bound on the context length required to faithfully sample length-$n$ sequences. In contrast, we prove that an autoregressive \emph{reasoning} model with only $Θ(\log n)$ working memory can sample exactly from the true language -- an exponential improvement. We confirm both the lower-bound predictions and the reasoning-based upper bound empirically with transformers trained on the synthetic language; the trained models track our asymptotic predictions quantitatively across a wide range of context sizes.

</details>


### [167] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究策略分类中个体为影响分类器而修改特征的成本结构问题。发现即使VC维为1的假设类，在简单区间邻域下也会导致诱导类VC维无限。通过引入几何可定义性假设（一阶逻辑公式在R_exp上定义），证明了可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究策略分类中诱导假设类的样本复杂度与原始假设类复杂度及成本结构之间的关系，尤其是揭示在何种条件下策略行为会导致学习问题从简单变为不可学习。

Method: 先通过反例证明一般情形下VC维1的假设类在简单区间邻域下诱导类VC维无限；再引入几何可定义性假设（基于R_exp上的一阶逻辑公式），在此假设下证明可学习性保持。

Result: 一般情形下策略行为可导致不可学习性；但在几何可定义性假设下（涵盖ℓ_p距离、Wasserstein距离、信息论散度等），可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由公式复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 策略分类的可学习性在一般情形下没有保障，但通过几何可定义性假设引入结构，可以在广泛的自然类与成本函数下恢复可学习性。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [168] [Rescaled Asynchronous SGD: Optimal Distributed Optimization under Data and System Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13434)
*Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rescaled ASGD方法，通过按计算时间重新缩放各工作节点的学习率，在保持标准异步SGD框架的同时，解决了数据异构分布下梯度更新有偏的问题，并证明了其在非凸设置下收敛到正确全局目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准异步SGD在本地数据分布异构时，因快慢节点贡献梯度数量不同，导致模型偏向频率加权平均的局部目标而非全局目标，现有修复方法复杂且偏离简单ASGD框架。

Method: 保持标准ASGD机制不变，根据各工作节点的计算时间重新缩放其步长，使得每个节点在一个周期内贡献相同的聚合学习率，从而恢复对正确全局目标的优化。

Result: 在非凸、光滑和有界异构假设下，证明Rescaled ASGD收敛到正确全局目标的驻点，时间复杂度在前导项达到已知下界，滞后性和数据异构性仅影响低阶项。实验表明该方法收敛到正确目标且与先进基线方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 无需引入复杂的缓冲或内存机制，仅通过重新缩放步长即可在标准ASGD框架下解决异构数据分布带来的偏差问题，是一种简洁有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD) is a standard way to exploit heterogeneous compute resources in distributed learning: instead of forcing fast workers to wait for slow ones, the server updates the model whenever a gradient arrives. Vanilla ASGD applies each arriving gradient with the same weight. When local data distributions are heterogeneous, this becomes problematic: faster workers contribute more updates, and we show theoretically that the method is biased toward a frequency-weighted average of the local objectives rather than the desired global objective. Existing remedies typically move away from the simple ASGD template by introducing gathering phases, buffering, or extra memory. We show that this is unnecessary. Keeping the standard ASGD mechanism, we recover the correct objective by rescaling worker-specific stepsizes in proportion to their computation times, so that each worker contributes the same aggregate learning rate over a cycle. In the non-convex setting, under smoothness and bounded heterogeneity assumptions, we prove that the resulting method, Rescaled ASGD, converges to stationary points of the correct global objective in the fixed-computation model. Its time complexity matches the known lower bound in the leading term, while the effects of staleness and data heterogeneity appear only in lower-order terms. Experiments confirm that the method converges to the correct objective and is competitive with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [169] [Efficient Sensor Fusion for Gesture Recognition on Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13462)
*Pietro Bartoli,Christian Veronesi,Tommaso Bondini,Andrea Giudici,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于低分辨率ToF与IR热传感器融合的轻量级手势识别系统，在MCU上实现92.3%准确率、50mW功耗和毫秒级推理延迟，适用于智能眼镜等资源受限的可穿戴设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于视觉的手势识别在功耗、计算延迟和用户隐私方面存在显著挑战，亟需一种轻量级、隐私保护的替代方案用于智能眼镜等HCI场景。

Method: 融合8×8多区域ToF传感器(VL53L8CH)和8×8红外热阵列(AMG8833)获取互补深度与热信息，设计紧凑型CNN（含分组卷积架构）在MCU上高效融合多模态数据。

Result: 在7种静态手势数据集上，融合策略准确率92.3%、宏F1-score 0.93；模型仅6,343参数，在STM32F4/H7上推理延迟毫秒级，总功耗50mW。

Conclusion: 该融合方案显著优于单传感器基线，验证了在资源受限可穿戴设备上实现高效、隐私保护手势识别的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Gesture recognition is a cornerstone of Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) for smart eyewear, enabling natural and device-free control in augmented reality environments. Traditional vision-based approaches face significant challenges regarding power consumption, computational latency, and user privacy. This paper proposes a lightweight, privacy-preserving gesture recognition system based on the fusion of low-resolution Time-of-Flight (ToF) and Infrared (IR) thermal sensors. We used an 8 times 8 multizone ToF sensor (VL53L8CH) and an 8 times 8 IR array (AMG8833) to capture complementary depth and thermal cues. A compact Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) with a specialized grouped-convolution architecture is designed to fuse these modalities efficiently on a microcontroller (MCU). Experimental results on a custom dataset of 7 static gestures, validated via k-fold cross-validation, demonstrate that the proposed fusion strategy significantly outperforms single-sensor baselines with an accuracy of 92.3% and a macro F1-score of 0.93. Finally, on-device benchmarks on STM32F4 and STM32H7 MCUs confirm the system's suitability for resource-constrained wearables, requiring only 6,343 parameters and achieving millisecond-level inference latency with a total system power of 50 mW.

</details>


### [170] [A Unified Three-Stage Machine Learning Framework for Diabetes Detection, Subtype Discrimination, and Cognitive-Metabolic Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13464)
*Vishal Pandey,Ruzina Haque Laskar,Rishav Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的三阶段机器学习框架，用于糖尿病检测、亚型聚类及代谢-认知关联分析。在NCSU数据集上，SVM-RBF和逻辑回归达到最高ROC-AUC（0.825），并通过SHAP识别出葡萄糖、BMI和年龄为关键预测因子；K-Means聚类恢复了临床合理的亚型分区；在俄亥俄纵向认知数据集上发现血糖控制与认知功能存在显著正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有糖尿病预测研究多局限于二分类问题，缺乏亚型导向分析和血糖-认知关联探索。

Method: 三阶段框架：阶段1使用五种监督分类器+堆叠集成进行糖尿病检测；阶段2对确诊患者进行K-Means聚类（k=2）实现亚型分区；阶段3对纵向认知数据进行血糖与认知功能的统计关联分析。

Result: SVM-RBF和逻辑回归ROC-AUC达0.825；随机森林准确率最高（0.762）；SHAP显示葡萄糖、BMI、年龄为关键生物标志物；聚类轮廓系数约0.116；血糖控制与认知功能显著正相关（ρ_s=0.208, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了统计可解释的ML流程在可复现糖尿病分析及亚型探索中的实用性。

Abstract: Diabetes mellitus affects over 537 million adults worldwide and remains a major challenge in preventive healthcare. Existing machine-learning studies primarily formulate diabetes prediction as a binary classification problem, while subtype-oriented analysis and glycaemic-cognitive associations remain comparatively underexplored. We present a reproducible three-stage machine learning framework for diabetes detection, subtype-oriented clustering, and metabolic-cognitive association analysis. In Stage 1, five supervised classifiers together with a stacking ensemble are benchmarked on the NCSU Diabetes Dataset using stratified five-fold cross-validation and evaluation metrics including ROC-AUC, balanced accuracy, recall, and F1-score. SVM-RBF and Logistic Regression achieve the highest ROC-AUC ($0.825 \pm 0.026$), while Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy ($0.762 \pm 0.030$). SHAP explainability identifies Glucose, BMI, and Age as the dominant predictive biomarkers. In Stage 2, silhouette-validated K-Means clustering ($k=2$, silhouette $\approx 0.116$) is applied to confirmed diabetic cases using Glucose, Insulin, and Age, recovering clinically plausible subtype-oriented partitions without requiring ground-truth subtype labels. In Stage 3, statistical analysis of the Ohio Longitudinal Cognitive Dataset ($n=373$) reveals a significant positive association between glycaemic control and cognitive function ($ρ_s = 0.208$, $p = 5.29 \times 10^{-5}$), which survives Holm correction. The findings support the utility of statistically grounded and interpretable ML pipelines for reproducible diabetes analytics and subtype-aware exploratory analysis.

</details>


### [171] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量化矩阵乘法，重点探讨在协方差矩阵已知情况下的权重量化，提出基于水填充（waterfilling）的改进方法，并证明WaterSIC方案在高速率下的性能接近理论极限，且GPTQ结合随机旋转也接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM量化算法（如GPTQ）在权重量化时平等分配比特率，未充分利用协方差信息，导致量化效率不高。本文旨在利用经典水填充理论改进这一分配策略。

Method: 将权重量化建模为加权均方误差（WMSE）源编码问题，采用反向水填充（reverse waterfilling）方法分配比特率；分析仅使用标量INT量化器的WaterSIC方案，并推导其高速率下的性能边界；与GPTQ进行对比实验。

Result: WaterSIC方案在高速率下：（a）具有基无关性，不受随机旋转影响；（b）与信息论失真极限仅差常数因子$\frac{2\pi e}{12}$（约0.25 bit/entry）。GPTQ在随机旋转和真实Llama-3-8B协方差下，性能与WaterSIC差距在0.1 bit以内。

Conclusion: 基于水填充的权重量化策略（如WaterSIC）性能优异且具有理论保证；GPTQ结合随机旋转同样接近最优，验证了实际LLM量化算法在高速率下的有效性。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [172] [Twincher: Bijective Representation Learning for Robust Inversion of Continuous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13470)
*Arkady Gonoskov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Twincher架构，通过可逆微分同胚变换和对抗训练学习前向过程的双射表示，实现鲁棒且高效的逆向推理，在数据效率和鲁棒性上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI主要由大规模神经架构驱动，缺乏针对资源高效感知与规划的归纳偏置，尤其在解决逆问题时表现不足。因此需要一种能够学习鲁棒双射表示的方法，使前向过程的输出与输入保持双向对齐，同时对噪声和模型失配具有不敏感性。

Method: 提出Twincher架构，基于堆叠的结构化微分同胚变换（可逆变换），结合定制的对抗训练策略，以学习y与p之间的双射表示。

Result: 在合成系统上，Twincher能够高效学习双射表示，实现鲁棒且高效的迭代逆推理。与基线逆模型方法相比，表现出更好的数据效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双射表示学习在机器人、视觉和物理AI领域具有潜力，Twincher为此提供了初步的实验证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have been primarily driven by large-scale neural architectures that excel at function approximation, rather than by tailored inductive biases and inference or learning strategies that could be important for resource-efficient real-world perception and planning through the solution of inverse problems. In this work, we consider the possibility of enabling robust inversion of continuous forward processes $p \mapsto y$ by learning representations of $y$ that are bijectively aligned with $p$ while remaining insensitive to perturbations in $y$ caused by noise or model mismatch. We propose Twincher, a class of architectures based on stacks of structured diffeomorphic transformations and tailored adversarial training strategies that enable learning such bijective representations. We provide a public API for training and inference and empirically demonstrate the ability of the proposed architecture to efficiently learn bijective representations of synthetic systems, thereby enabling robust and efficient iterative inverse inference. Compared to a baseline inverse-modeling approach, the method exhibits improved data efficiency and robustness, providing initial evidence for the potential of bijective representation learning in robotics, vision, and physical AI.

</details>


### [173] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MinT是一个专为LoRA微调与在线推理设计的托管基础设施系统，支持在共享千亿级基座模型上管理和服务百万级LoRA策略目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要将每个LoRA策略物化为完整检查点，资源开销巨大，难以在多策略场景下高效训练和服务。

Method: MinT通过三个维度扩展：Scale Up支持前沿稠密和MoE架构的LoRA强化学习；Scale Down仅传输不足基座模型1%的LoRA适配器，减少18.3x到2.85x的步骤开销；Scale Out实现百万级可寻址策略目录和千级适配器并发加载。

Result: 在4B稠密模型上适配器交接加速18.3x，30B MoE模型加速2.85x；多策略GRPO墙钟时间缩短1.77x和1.45x；打包MoE LoRA张量提升引擎加载速度8.5-8.7x。

Conclusion: MinT有效管理百万级LoRA策略目录，同时支持在共享的万亿参数基座模型上训练和服务选定的适配器版本。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [174] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ensembits，首个蛋白质构象集合的tokenizer，通过残差VQ-VAE和帧蒸馏目标在分子动力学语料上训练，在动态预测和静态任务上均表现优异，并能从单结构预测动态token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构tokenizer仅捕捉静态结构的局部几何特征，忽略了蛋白质集合所揭示的关联运动和交替构象状态，缺乏对蛋白质动态信息的编码能力。

Method: 采用残差VQ-VAE架构，结合帧蒸馏目标（frame distillation objective），在大规模分子动力学语料上训练。设计跨构象的几何描述符、置换不变编码以及处理动态数据稀疏性的策略。

Result: 在RMSF预测上优于所有相关方法；在基于token的ANOVA测试中，是每残基运动振幅最强的独立结构tokenizer；在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测及零样本突变效应预测上匹配或超越静态tokenizer，且预训练数据量少得多。

Conclusion: 蒸馏目标使Ensembits能从单预测结构预测动态token，缓解动态数据稀疏问题。随着领域从静态结构预测转向集合生成，Ensembits为将动力学引入蛋白质语言模型和设计提供了离散词汇表。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [175] [Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13790)
*Zhonghao Li,Chaoyu Liu,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Di-BiLPS统一神经框架，结合VAE、潜在扩散模型和对比学习，在极稀疏观测（低至3%）下高效求解PDE正/逆问题，实现SOTA性能和零样本超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际PDE应用中观测数据极度稀疏，现有数值求解器和神经方法在极稀疏场景下精度严重下降、推理效率受限。

Method: 将VAE压缩高维输入到潜在空间，结合潜在扩散模块建模不确定性，引入对比学习对齐表征，并设计基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE信息去噪算法。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，极稀疏输入（低至3%）下达到SOTA性能，大幅降低计算成本，并支持零样本超分辨率。

Conclusion: Di-BiLPS有效解决了极稀疏观测下的PDE求解问题，兼顾精度与效率，具备连续时空域预测能力。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are fundamental for modeling complex natural and physical phenomena. In many real-world applications, however, observational data are extremely sparse, which severely limits the applicability of both classical numerical solvers and existing neural approaches. While neural methods have shown promising results under moderately sparse observations, their inference efficiency at high resolutions is limited, and their accuracy degrades substantially in the extremely sparse regime. In this work, we propose the Di-BiLPS, a unified neural framework that effectively handle both forward and inverse PDE problems under extremely sparse observations. Di-BiLPS combines a variational autoencoder to compress high-dimensional inputs into a compact latent space, a latent diffusion module to model uncertainty, and contrastive learning to align representations. Operating entirely in this latent space, the framework achieves efficient inference while retaining flexible input-output mapping. In addition, we introduce a PDE-informed denoising algorithm based on a variance-preserving diffusion process, which further improves inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple PDE benchmarks demonstrate that Di-BiLPS consistently achieves SOTA performance under extremely sparse inputs (as low as 3%), while substantially reducing computational cost. Moreover, Di-BiLPS enables zero-shot super-resolution, as it allows predictions over continuous spatial-temporal domains.

</details>


### [176] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM），将标准Flow Matching推广到多参数场景，实现路径无关的分布间传输，并能在合适假设下逼近Wasserstein重心，实验表明PiFM在合成和真实数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Flow Matching是单参数框架，无法处理多参数变化场景中路径无关的传输需求。路径无关性确保变换仅依赖于初始和目标分布，而非具体路径，这在许多实际应用中至关重要。

Method: PiFM通过将Flow Matching推广到高维参数域，施加结构条件保证组合变换的一致性。提出了一种可训练的、免模拟的目标函数，用于回归多参数条件概率路径，并证明在适当假设下PiFM可近似Wasserstein重心。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，PiFM在插值路径无关轨迹和生成所需分布外样本方面均优于其他方法。

Conclusion: PiFM有效解决了多参数场景下的路径无关分布传输问题，将Flow Matching与Wasserstein重心联系起来，提供了理论保障和实用的训练方法。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [177] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层次自助法来建模标注者行为，通过分析项目数量(N)和每项回复数量(K)之间的权衡，以解决AI评估中的可重复性危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型日益普及，但面临可重复性危机——人工评估存在偏差和主观性，且缺乏研究标注者数量如何影响实验可重复性的数据。

Method: 提出多层次自助法（multi-level bootstrapping approach），利用大量标注数据和持久标注者标识符，建模标注者行为并分析N与K的权衡关系。

Result: 通过分析N（项目数量）和K（每项回复数量）的权衡，找到实现统计显著性所需的最优配置。

Conclusion: 该方法有助于克服人工评估中的方差问题，提升AI评估的可重复性和可信度。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [178] [Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13834)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Tao Zhong,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角研究几何网格上的物理场方程求解算子，提出基于Hodge正交性的谱对偶架构（HSD），通过离散微分形式与正交辅助空间分离拓扑与几何成分，实现了高精度、高效率且保持物理不变量的几何图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在几何网格上求解物理场方程时，难以区分不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学，导致频谱干扰和物理不变量损失。

Method: 基于Hodge理论与算子分裂，推导算子级分解；提出混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构，利用离散微分形式捕获拓扑主导成分，并设计正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学。

Result: 在几何图上实现了更优越的精度和效率，并增强了物理不变量的保真度。

Conclusion: HSD架构通过代数层面的归纳偏置（Hodge谱对偶）有效解决了频谱干扰问题，为几何图上的物理场建模提供了结构保持的新范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality

</details>


### [179] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过U-Net模型融合静止轨道卫星(SEVIRI/MSG)与极轨卫星(Terra/Aqua MODIS)数据，生成了高时空分辨率(1km, 15分钟间隔)的地表温度产品，并基于ConvLSTM架构实现了15-75分钟的地表温度预报，在RMSE和偏差指标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星LST产品存在空间分辨率与时间分辨率之间的根本权衡，无法同时满足高空间和高时间分辨率的需求。

Method: 1) 使用U-Net模型将SEVIRI/MSG的LST场(3km, 15分钟)映射到MODIS的LST场(1km, 每日4次过境)；2) 基于ConvLSTM架构构建LST临近预报模型，预报提前时间为15-75分钟。

Result: U-Net模型在测试集上RMSE=1.92°C，MBE=0.01°C；ConvLSTM预报模型在不同提前时间下RMSE为0.57-1.15°C，偏差为-0.1至0.14°C，优于持久性和气候滚动中位数基准。

Conclusion: 该高时空分辨率LST预报模型可直接应用于业务化卫星LST监测，解决时空分辨率权衡问题。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [180] [Achieving $ε^{-2}$ Sample Complexity for Single-Loop Actor-Critic under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13639)
*Ishaq Hamza,Zaiwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单循环、单时间尺度的离策略演员-评论家方法在极弱假设（仅需存在一个能生成不可约马尔可夫链的策略）下具有$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-2})$的样本复杂度，首次实现了与嵌套循环方法相当的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离策略演员-评论家方法要么依赖嵌套循环和强假设（如均匀混合、均匀探索）才能达到最优样本复杂度，要么在单循环实现下缺乏理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出耦合Lyapunov漂移框架：分别建立演员的几何收敛率和评论家的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$收敛率，并通过交叉支配性质将二者结合，从而处理单循环实现中耦合更新方程和离策略学习带来的无界迭代问题。

Result: 在仅需存在一个能生成不可约马尔可夫链的策略这一最小假设下，证明了单循环、单时间尺度离策略演员-评论家方法的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-2})$样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在极弱假设下实现了与嵌套循环方法相同的最优样本复杂度，且提出的耦合Lyapunov漂移框架可推广至其他具有无界迭代的耦合迭代算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for off-policy actor--critic methods in reinforcement learning. In particular, under a single-loop, single-timescale implementation and a broad class of policy updates, including approximate policy iteration and natural policy gradient methods, we prove the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantee for finding an $ε$-optimal policy under minimal assumptions, namely, the existence of a policy that induces an irreducible Markov chain. This stands in stark contrast to the existing literature, where an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity is achieved only through nested-loop updates and/or under strong, algorithm-dependent assumptions on the policies, such as uniform mixing and uniform exploration. Technically, to address the challenges posed by the coupled update equations arising from the single-loop implementation, as well as the potentially unbounded iterates induced by off-policy learning, our analysis is based on a coupled Lyapunov drift framework. Specifically, we establish a geometric convergence rate for the actor and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ convergence rate for the critic, and combine the two Lyapunov drift inequalities through a cross-domination property. We believe this analytical framework is of independent interest and may be applicable to other coupled iterative algorithms with unbounded

</details>


### [181] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于后验预测的采样器用于流语言模型（FLM），通过从因子化后验分布中采样干净端点，再以奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克桥条件采样连续状态，无需额外训练即可提升生成质量-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDPM式采样器将FLM的边际后验分布坍缩为单一条件均值端点，该端点通常不是有效的一热序列，导致生成质量受限。本文旨在设计一种更自然的采样策略。

Method: 提出后验预测采样器：每步逆过程中，从FLM token边际定义的因子化后验中采样干净的一热端点，再以该端点为条件从解析的奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克桥中采样下一连续状态。该方法无需训练，且支持温度缩放和核截断等解码控制。

Result: 证明在精确后验边际下，端点近似误差等于token位置间的条件多信息；一步桥核保留了所有token-wise后验预测边际，仅损失残差交叉位置依赖；Girsanov路径空间比较表明边际条件桥的降噪误差项不大于冻结条件均值桥，且在中间观测提供额外信息时严格更优。

Conclusion: 实验表明该采样器改善了生成质量与多样性之间的权衡，为FLM提供了一种更 principled 的采样框架。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [182] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了实值函数类在尺度γ下的一致收敛、γ/2下的不可知学习性与脂肪破碎维数有限性三者等价性定理，解决了Anthony和Bartlett（1999）提出的尺度精确性问题，并推翻了Phil Long关于乘法2因子不可避免的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Anthony和Bartlett（1999）提出的关于学习性精确尺度控制的开放问题，反驳Phil Long关于2倍因子差距不可避免的猜想，并改进Bartlett和Long（1998）的上界。

Method: 关键技术创新是直接对经验ℓ∞覆盖数进行上界估计，绕过了传统的通过填充数的迂回路径。由此获得了关于脂肪破碎尺度γ的渐近度量熵紧界。

Result: 在γ/2尺度上得到O(log² n)界（有时紧），在2γ尺度上得到O(log n)界。解决了Alon等（1997）和Rudelson与Vershynin（2006）的开放问题。还建立了有界积分概率度量（IPM）的尖锐二分法：要么可估计，要么对任意c<3因子无法弱评估，而3-弱评估总是成立，解决了Aiyer等（2026）的开放问题。

Conclusion: 建立了尺度敏感的PAC学习基本定理的推广，为实值函数类的学习性提供了精确的尺度刻画，并提出了若干关于量化样本复杂度和可评估性的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [183] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合动作在线决策问题中遗憾（regret）与多面体不稳定性（区域切换次数）之间的关系，提出了一个统一的遗憾界 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))，并在次模-凹博弈中将其具体化为排列切换次数，实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合动作空间的在线决策问题通常通过凸松弛转化为在线凸优化（OCO）问题，但现有遗憾界未能充分刻画组合结构带来的特殊性质。作者希望揭示遗憾与多面体区域切换次数之间的内在联系，从而在不同问题间建立统一的遗憾分析框架。

Method: 理论证明：在全信息反馈和固定划分假设下，证明遗憾界 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))，其中 RS_T 是区域切换次数，V_max 是每个区域的最大顶点数。在在线次模-凹博弈中，利用Lovász凸化将问题转化为排列切换次数 SC_T，得到匹配率 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))。实验验证：在最短路径和影响力最大化等组合问题上进行合成和真实数据实验。

Result: 理论结果：遗憾界在专家型（小RS_T）和维度依赖型OCO率之间平滑插值，当区域切换次数较小时达到更优的遗憾。实验结果表明，实际中低不稳定性（低切换次数）场景确实存在，无需显式枚举所有动作即可获得更好的性能。

Conclusion: 多面体不稳定性（区域切换次数）是组合动作在线决策问题中遗憾的核心决定因素。这一发现为设计更高效的在线学习算法提供了新视角，尤其在动作空间巨大但实际切换次数较低的场景中具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure. We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [184] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MILM模型，将多模态不规则时间序列转化为XML格式，通过两阶段微调LLM进行序列分类，在EHR数据集上取得最优表现，并证明采样模式本身携带预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）数据包含不同步、不规则采样的数值和文本通道（如EHR中的实验室测量和临床记录），采样的时间与通道模式本身携带预测信号。现有方法难以同时处理异构数值与文本数据，而LLM具备丰富的预训练知识，适合处理此类异构数据。

Method: 将MITS表示为时间排序的XML格式三元组；采用两阶段微调策略：第一阶段在值被遮蔽的MITS上训练，让模型仅从采样模式中学习预测；第二阶段在完整MITS上训练，联合建模采样模式与观测值。单阶段版本MILM-Direct作为对比。

Result: MILM-2S（两阶段）和MILM-Direct在多个EHR数据集上分别取得最佳和次佳平均性能。值遮蔽实验证实采样模式携带预测信号，且MILM-2S能有效利用该信号。在值缺失评估中，MILM-2S相比MILM-Direct优势更大。

Conclusion: MILM-2S通过两阶段训练有效利用了MITS中的采样模式信息，在值缺失场景下表现尤为突出，表明保留缺失观测的时间和通道信息可进一步提升院内死亡率预测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [185] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对熵风险度量下的有限视界风险敏感强化学习的最佳策略识别问题，通过提出基于KL探索奖励的前向模型算法，并结合指数效用函数的平滑性推导出更紧的浓度界和新的停止规则，将样本复杂度从O(e^{2|β|H})降至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，解决了指数视界依赖的常数差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作在下界Ω(e^{|β|H})与上界O(e^{2|β|H})之间存在指数视界依赖的常数差距，作者希望弥合这一差距，找到与下界匹配的最优样本复杂度上界。

Method: 提出基于前向模型的算法，利用KL散度探索奖励适配熵准则；利用指数效用函数的平滑性导出更紧的浓度界；设计新的停止规则进一步利用这种紧致性。

Result: 样本复杂度从O(e^{2|β|H})提升至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，即消除了额外的指数因子。

Conclusion: 通过更精细的浓度控制和创新的停止规则，成功解决了风险敏感强化学习最佳策略识别中指数视界依赖的常数差距问题，实现了样本复杂度的最优性。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [186] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI虽能提升学习表现，但未能促进深度认知和元认知加工，限制了高质量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI在教育中的应用主要关注提升学习表现，忽视了深层认知与元认知过程对高质量学习的重要性。

Method: 通过分析生成式AI在教育中的现有应用方式，指出其缺乏对深度认知和元认知加工的支持。

Result: 生成式AI的使用未能促进学习者进行深度认知和元认知加工。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在教育中的应用需要重新设计，以更好地支持深度认知和元认知过程，从而实现高质量学习。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [187] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pinductor方法，利用LLM先验从少量观测-行动轨迹中高效学习POMDP世界模型，无需访问隐藏状态即可达到与有特权信息方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，智能体需要学习内部世界模型（POMDP），但传统方法需要大量环境交互。作者希望利用语言模型的先验知识来减少这种昂贵的交互成本。

Method: 提出Pinductor框架：由LLM从少量观测-行动轨迹中提出候选POMDP模型，然后通过迭代优化基于信念的似然分数来逐步精炼模型。

Result: Pinductor在性能和样本效率上匹配了需要访问隐藏状态的LLM-based POMDP方法，显著优于表格型POMDP基线。性能随LLM能力提升而提升，且当环境语义信息被隐藏时性能退化平缓。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验可以作为部分可观测环境下样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，为构建通用智能体提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [188] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GHGbench是一个开放数据集和基准测试，旨在解决公司层面和建筑层面温室气体排放预测中的数据碎片化问题。公司轨道包含32,000+条公司-年度记录，建筑轨道整合了491,591条建筑-年度记录。主要发现：建筑排放预测比公司更难；分布内与分布外差距远超模型间差距；多模态遥感嵌入在表格模型泛化失败时特别有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体级碳排放预测数据集和基准在访问、规模、粒度和评估方面存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的开放基准来系统评估不同方法的性能。

Method: 构建了双轨数据集（公司级和建筑级），定义了规范的数据划分（分布内、跨区域/城市迁移、时间留出、短期预测），建立了包含梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer和多模态融合等多种基线方法，并采用多种子配对自助法进行统计检验。

Result: (i) 建筑排放预测结构上比公司排放更难；(ii) 分布内到分布外的性能差距远超任何模型间差距，表格基础模型首次在多城市建筑排放任务上显著优于调优树；(iii) 多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效时特别有效。同时揭示了灾难性城市迁移和行业因子查找上限等系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: GHGbench为碳排放预测提供了统一的开放基准，揭示了当前方法的关键局限性（分布泛化差距、城市迁移失败、行业因子天花板），并指出了多模态融合作为有前景的方向。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction.
